(function() {
    var _getCookieAsJson = function(cname) {
        var cookie = _getCookie(cname);
        if (!cookie) {
            return {};
        }
        var json = {};
        var chunks = decodeURIComponent(cookie).split("&");
        for (var i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
            var chunk = chunks[i].split("=");
            json[chunk[0]] = chunk[1];
        }
        return json;
    };
    var _getCookie = function(cname) {
        var name = cname + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(";");
        for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == " ")
                c = c.substring(1);
            if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
        }

        return "";
    };
    var _getTimezone = function(obj) {
        var timezone = obj && obj["tz"] ? obj["tz"] : null;
        if (timezone && wslTimezoneData && wslTimezoneData[timezone]) {
            timezone = wslTimezoneData[timezone]["name"];
        } else {
            timezone = null;
        }
        return timezone;
    };
    var _getGender = function(obj) {
        var gender = obj && obj["g"] ? obj["g"] : null;
        if (gender && gender == "M") {
            gender = "male";
        } else if (gender) {
            gender = "female";
        }
        return gender;
    };
    var auCookie = _getCookieAsJson("au");
    var timezone = _getTimezone(auCookie, null);
    var gender = _getGender(auCookie, null);
    var userId = auCookie && auCookie["u"] ? auCookie["u"] : null;
    var yearBorn = auCookie && auCookie["by"] ? auCookie["by"] : null;
    var language = auCookie && auCookie["l"] ? auCookie["l"] : null;
    var country = auCookie && auCookie["c"] ? auCookie["c"] : null;
    var joinDate = auCookie && auCookie["rd"] ? auCookie["rd"] : null;
    var loggedIn = userId ? "true" : "false";
    var notifySurfingNews = null;
    var notifyLiveEvents = null;
    var notifyFantasy = null;
    var notifyPartnerOffers = null;
    var userInfo = {
        "user-logged-in": loggedIn
    };
    var udCookie = Cookies.getJSON("ud");
    if (udCookie) {
        if (udCookie.a) {
            notifySurfingNews = udCookie.a.indexOf("content") !== -1 ? "true" : "false";
            notifyLiveEvents = udCookie.a.indexOf("events") !== -1 ? "true" : "false";
            notifyFantasy = udCookie.a.indexOf("fantasy") !== -1 ? "true" : "false";
            notifyPartnerOffers = udCookie.a.indexOf("partners") !== -1 ? "true" : "false";
        }
    }
    if (userId) {
        userInfo["user-id"] = userId;
    }
    if (timezone) {
        userInfo["user-timezone"] = timezone;
    }
    if (gender) {
        userInfo["user-gender"] = gender;
    }
    if (yearBorn) {
        userInfo["user-birth-year"] = yearBorn;
    }
    if (language) {
        userInfo["user-language"] = language;
    }
    if (country) {
        userInfo["user-country"] = country;
    }
    if (joinDate) {
        userInfo["user-join-date"] = joinDate;
    }
    if (userId) {
        userInfo["user-notifications"] = {
            "surfing-news": notifySurfingNews,
            "live-events": notifyLiveEvents,
            fantasy: notifyFantasy,
            "partner-offers": notifyPartnerOffers
        };
    }
    var loggedIn = userId ? "true" : "false";
    var gtmEvents = [{"event":"dataLayer-initialized","site":{"site-version":"165522a","site-layout":"Desktop","site-deployment-status":"Production"},"page":{"page-type":"home","screen-name":"home","content-type":"hub","content-id":"23567","content-title":"Latest News Hub","slug":"content-23567","url":"\/","location":"https:\/\/www.worldsurfleague.com\/","page-title":"World Surf League - The global home of surfing"},"append-user-info":true}];
    for (var i = 0; i < gtmEvents.length; i++) {
        var gtmEvent = gtmEvents[i];
        if ( gtmEvent.event == "dataLayer-initialized" && gtmEvent.page  ) {
            gtmEvent.page.location = window.location.href;
        }
        var dataLayerPayload = Object.assign({}, { event: gtmEvent.event }, gtmEvent.page, userInfo, gtmEvent.site);
        var eventNameIndex = gtmEvent.event == "dataLayer-initialized" ? "global-virtual-pageview" : gtmEvent.event;
        window.dataLayer.push(dataLayerPayload);
        window.dataLayerByEvent[eventNameIndex] = dataLayerPayload;
    }
})();
</script>
